### [205] [Decoupling Memory from Context: Structured Memory for Token-Efficient Test-Time Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02687)
*Yehya Farhat,Michael Desmond,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体记忆系统更新统一表述为上下文优化过程，并提出GraphMemory——一种轻量级图结构记忆，通过累积、精炼、组织可复用策略并按需检索相关子图，在检索有界下使记忆量随样本增长保持恒定，性能具竞争力且记忆构建Token减少约81-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在企业、科研与医疗等场景需融合领域知识并从经验中适应。上下文工程是权重更新之外的实用替代方案，但在线适配上下文常依赖高成本试错，且查询多被独立处理，经验难以累积。记忆系统虽可跨交互保留信息，但持续向共享上下文追加信息会导致Token开销上升、上下文窗口受限以及随上下文扩张而性能退化。

Method: 作者首先给出上下文优化的统一表述，论证智能体记忆系统的更新可解释为对模型上下文的优化更新过程，为记忆设计与效率研究提供原则性框架。随后提出GraphMemory：一种轻量级图结构记忆，能够累积、精炼、组织并连接可复用策略；针对每个查询仅检索相关子图，实现无需将全部记忆暴露给模型的在线上下文适配。

Result: 在检索有界的设定下，随着已处理样本数量增长，被检索的记忆量保持恒定。实验表明GraphMemory取得具竞争力的下游性能，同时记忆构建Token相比基线减少约81-85%。

Conclusion: 将记忆更新视为上下文优化为记忆机制提供了统一的分析视角；GraphMemory以图结构组织与子图检索实现了高效且可扩展的在线上下文适配，在性能与Token成本间取得良好平衡。该工作为记忆设计原则与效率研究提供了框架与实证支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise, scientific, and medical applications, where agents must incorporate domain-specific knowledge and adapt from experience. Context engineering offers a practical alternative to weight updates by improving model behavior through instructions, strategies, and evidence supplied at inference time. However, adapting context online typically requires a costly trial-and-error process, while queries are often processed independently, preventing useful experience from carrying forward. Memory systems address this limitation by retaining information across interactions, but approaches that continually append information to a shared context face increasing token costs, context-window limits, and performance degradation as the context expands. We introduce a unified formulation of context optimization and show that an agent memory system update can be interpreted as an optimization update procedure over the model's context. This perspective attempts to provide a principled framework for studying memory design and its efficiency. We then propose GraphMemory, a lightweight graph-based memory that accumulates, refines, organizes, and connects reusable strategies. For each query, GraphMemory retrieves only the relevant subgraph, enabling online context adaptation without exposing the model to the entire memory. Under bounded retrieval, the amount of retrieved memory remains constant as the number of processed examples grows. Experiments show that GraphMemory achieves competitive downstream performance while using approximately 81-85% fewer memory-construction tokens than our baselines.

</details>


### [206] [Learning to Revise Reasoning with Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02703)
*Yuxiang Zhang,Ding Cao,Shuting Cui,Lei Wang,Weijieying Ren,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation (Seg-OPD)，将教师重写片段转化为显式监督以学习推理修改，在数学推理与竞赛编程任务上平均相对提升5.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token-wise OPD仅在学生自身rollout上提供密集token级监督，无法显式给出连贯的替代推理步骤来说明学生步骤应如何修改；且当学生产生退化推理前缀时，教师监督仍以该前缀为条件，可能强化错误推理模式。

Method: 通过受控推理干预发现用教师重写片段替换学生片段能提升后续推理准确性。据此提出Seg-OPD：基于不确定性指标选择学生片段，获取对应的教师重写片段，训练学生偏好教师重写而非配对的学生片段，同时保留密集token-wise OPD监督。

Result: 在数学推理与竞赛编程任务上，Seg-OPD训练的学生取得更高的修改成功率；在多种模型与任务上一致优于对比的SOTA基线，平均相对提升5.22%。

Conclusion: 将教师重写作为片段级显式监督、并结合token级OPD，能有效学习推理修改，缓解退化前缀带来的监督问题，提升推理准确性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves large language model reasoning by training students on their own rollouts with dense token-wise supervision from the teacher. However, token-wise OPD does not explicitly provide a coherent alternative reasoning step showing how the student's step could be revised to improve subsequent reasoning. Furthermore, this paradigm can become less effective when the student produces a degenerate reasoning prefix, as subsequent teacher supervision remains conditioned on that prefix and may reinforce poor reasoning patterns. In this work, we focus on learning reasoning revision with segment-wise OPD to rework intermediate reasoning steps and better support subsequent reasoning. Through controlled reasoning interventions, we find that replacing student segments with teacher redrafts improves subsequent reasoning accuracy. Therefore, we address the problem of turning teacher redrafts into explicit supervision for learning to revise reasoning. We propose Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation (Seg-OPD), which selects student segments based on an uncertainty metric and obtains corresponding teacher redrafts. Seg-OPD trains the student to prefer teacher redrafts over their paired student segments while retaining dense token-wise OPD supervision. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and competitive programming tasks show that Seg-OPD-trained students achieve higher revision success rates than baselines. Seg-OPD consistently outperforms the compared state-of-the-art baselines in reasoning accuracy with an average relative improvement of 5.22% across diverse models and tasks. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Seg-OPD.

</details>


### [207] [Label-Efficient Time Series Classification at Scale: A Dual-Stream OSSE-LSTM with Counterfactual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02704)
*Nguyen Ho,Bach Tung Tran,Trung Ky Nguyen,Zhenchang Xia,Bolong Zheng,Long Van Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双流OSSE-LSTM小样本时序分类框架，结合多尺度卷积与双向LSTM，并引入反事实积分梯度实现可解释的原型度量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模时序分析的核心瓶颈是标注成本而非数据量，实际问题是每类需标注多少样本才能获得可用分类器。作者研究标签空间固定且已知、仅用每类K个标注样本构建决策规则的场景。

Method: 提出OSSE-LSTM情节式度量学习框架：一支为Omni-Scale CNN加Squeeze-and-Excitation重校准，用于无逐数据集核调参的多尺度motif提取；另一支为双向LSTM捕捉全局时序上下文；两流独立归一化后融合为面向原型的嵌入。并引入反事实积分梯度(C-IG)，对目标类与对立类之间的原型间隔进行归因，并将归因图作为软掩码在测试时精炼原型而不更新编码器。

Result: 在19个单变量UCR数据集上，OSSE-LSTM在所有支持集规模下均取得最高平均准确率与逐数据集胜出次数；该范围内准确率波动仅0.36个百分点(96.36–96.72%)；其最弱配置仍超过所有对比基线在任意K下的最佳结果(93.99%)。

Conclusion: OSSE-LSTM在极少标注样本下实现稳定且领先的时序分类性能，且C-IG提供面向原型间隔的可解释性，兼顾精度与可解释性。

Abstract: Time series are produced continuously at enormous scale by industrial equipment, wearables, power grids, and clinical monitors, yet annotation remains manual, expensive, and expert-dependent. The binding constraint in large-scale time series analytics is therefore not data volume but label volume, and the question facing a practitioner is concrete: how many examples per class must be labeled before a classifier becomes usable? We study this question directly, in a regime where the label space is fixed and known in advance and the decision rule must be constructed from only K labeled examples per class. We propose Dual-Stream OSSE-LSTM, an episodic metric-learning framework that pairs an Omni-Scale CNN with Squeeze-and-Excitation recalibration, for multi-scale motif extraction without per-dataset kernel tuning, with a Bidirectional LSTM for global temporal context. The two streams are independently normalized and fused into a prototype-oriented embedding. Because decisions taken from a few labels must also be explainable, we introduce Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (C-IG), which attributes the prototype margin between target and opposing classes rather than an isolated classifier logit, and reuses the resulting maps as soft masks for test-time prototype refinement without updating the encoder. On 19 univariate UCR datasets, OSSE-LSTM attains the highest average accuracy and per-dataset win count at every support size, and its accuracy remains within a 0.36-point band (96.36-96.72%) across that range. Its weakest configuration still exceeding the best result any compared baseline achieves at any K (93.99%).

</details>


### [208] [Ego2World: Compiling Egocentric Cooking Videos into Executable Worlds for Belief-State Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02715)
*Qinchuan Cheng,Zhantao Gong,Pengzhan Sun,Angela Yao,Shijie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Ego2World基准，将第一人称烹饪活动注释编译为部分可观测下的可执行规划环境，用于评估智能体动作后果，揭示"操作被接受但目标未达成"的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频记录了日常活动，但评估智能体需考察其自主选择动作的后果，现有基准缺乏可执行、可检验结果的环境。

Method: Ego2World通过编译器将标注的烹饪步骤与对象链接为符号动作规则、持久世界状态和显式任务条件；世界状态与智能体信念分离维护，支持跨持续任务的规划与信息复用研究。对六个规划器在105个任务上评估，并开展配对Qwen-Plus研究。

Result: 六个规划器评估显示被接受的操作常未达成任务目标；执行轨迹与条件检查可区分中断、部分达成、执行完成但目标未达成三类情况。配对实验中持久信念使动作有效性提升4.15个百分点，视觉查询尝试减少90.27%，但token使用更高且未检测到完成度提升。

Conclusion: Ego2World提供了可复用测试平台，用于追踪规划与记忆选择如何影响执行、观测需求和任务达成，将人类活动记录与交互式智能体的开发评估相连接。

Abstract: Egocentric videos capture how people carry out everyday activities, yet testing an agent requires evaluating the consequences of actions it chooses itself. We introduce Ego2World, a benchmark that turns annotated cooking activities into executable planning environments under partial observation. Its compiler links source steps and objects to symbolic action rules, persistent world states, and explicit task conditions, so researchers can execute an agent's proposed actions and check their outcomes. World state and agent belief are maintained separately, enabling controlled studies of planning and information reuse across continuing tasks. Evaluating six planners on 105 tasks shows that accepted operations often leave task goals unmet. Execution traces and condition checks distinguish interrupted runs, partial attainment, and completed execution without goal attainment. In a separate paired Qwen-Plus study, persistent belief improves action validity by 4.15 percentage points and reduces visual-query attempts by 90.27%, with higher token use and no detected completion gain. Ego2World provides a reusable testbed for tracing how planning and memory choices affect execution, observation demand, and task attainment, connecting recorded human activity to the development and evaluation of interactive agents.

</details>


### [209] [On the Chain-of-Thought Monitorability of Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02741)
*Han Wang,Ishwar B Balappanawar,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估循环架构语言模型（LoopLM）的思维链（CoT）可监控性，发现更深循环深度在部分压力测试任务上会降低可监控性，但循环架构本身并不意味着可监控性更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是检测模型不良行为的有效手段；LoopLM通过重复应用共享Transformer层增加有效计算深度而不增大模型规模，但循环架构对CoT可监控性的影响尚缺乏研究。

Method: 在两种互补设定下评估：（1）同一LoopLM家族内改变循环深度，以隔离额外递归计算的影响；（2）将LoopLM与参数量、Transformer层数或有效深度匹配的非循环模型对比。实验覆盖MonitorBench的八个任务，采用标准与压力测试两种设置。

Result: 在特定逻辑/科学/工程类Cue Answer任务的压力测试中观察到任务相关的CoT可监控性下降，其他任务趋势较弱或性质不同。诊断表明该下降无法完全由任务难度、验证通过率或生成长度解释；定性样例显示更深循环模型对给定线索的使用或归因方式发生变化。跨模型对比未发现LoopLM系统性低于匹配的非循环模型。

Conclusion: 更深的循环深度在部分压力测试任务上可能降低CoT可监控性，但仅循环Transformer架构本身并不必然导致可监控性下降。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring provides a promising approach for detecting undesirable model behavior. Looped language models (LoopLMs) repeatedly apply shared transformer layers, increasing effective computational depth and enabling additional latent computation without increasing model size. However, the effect of looped architectures on CoT monitorability remains largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of CoT monitorability in LoopLMs. We study two complementary settings: (1) varying the loop depth within the same LoopLM family to isolate the effect of additional recurrent computation, and (2) comparing LoopLMs with non-looped language models matched by parameter size, transformer-layer count, or effective depth to study whether LoopLMs are less monitorable. Across eight tasks from MonitorBench and both standard and stress-test settings, we observe task-dependent reductions in CoT monitorability under stress tests on specific Logic/Science/Engineering \texttt{Cue Answer} tasks, while other tasks exhibit weaker or qualitatively different trends. Our diagnosis suggests that these declines are not fully explained by task difficulty, verification pass rate, or generated token length; qualitative examples further suggest changes in how deeper-loop models explicitly use or attribute provided cues. Our cross-model comparison finds no evidence that LoopLMs are systematically less monitorable than non-looped language models matched on size or depth. Overall, our results suggest that deeper loop depth can reduce CoT monitorability in some tasks under stress tests, but looped transformer architecture alone does not necessarily imply lower monitorability.

</details>


### [210] [Dynamic LLM Routers are Often Misguided](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02762)
*Sam Wang,Julia White,Sahibzada Allahyar,Dhruv Atreja,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了6个商用大模型路由器，发现在14种设置下它们均未能超越"在两个精选模型间随机选择"的基线；作者将差距归因于四种普遍模式，并指出标准评估目标本身在奖励这些模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态路由器被期望通过将每个查询分配给"能正确回答的最便宜模型"来降低推理成本，但其实际有效性缺乏系统性检验，尤其缺乏与合理基线的对比。

Method: 在覆盖8类任务的多样化基准上，对6个商用路由器在14种设置下进行评测，与成本匹配的两模型随机路由基线对比；分析路由器行为以识别失败模式；并从理论上分析标准目标函数（在已实现成本上的成本-准确率帕累托效率）的激励结构；最后提出替代评估方法并设计概念验证型两模型路由器。

Result: 没有任何商用路由器在成本匹配条件下优于随机两模型路由基线，部分路由器落后超过10个百分点。差距源于四种模式：难度盲区（difficulty blindness）、长度反转（length reversal）、语义匹配（semantic matching）与模型池次优（roster suboptimality）。前三种恰是标准目标所奖励的：倾向于升级中等难度而非最难查询、偏好更短而非更长查询、依据查询来源而非难度进行路由。支撑大模型池的两个假设——模型粒度与模型专精——在实证上均不成立。

Conclusion: 现有商用路由器存在系统性缺陷，且部分缺陷是当前评估目标的直接激励结果。作者提出不奖励这些模式的替代评估方法，并给出一个规避全部四种模式的两模型路由器作为概念验证；但其相对随机路由的增益有限，因为一个精心挑选的模型组合本身留下的路由空间很小。

Abstract: Dynamic LLM routers promise to cut inference costs by sending each query to the cheapest model that can answer it correctly. We analyze six commercial routers across 14 settings on a diverse benchmark spanning eight task categories, finding that none of them outperforms a router that randomly selects between two well-chosen models at matched cost. Some underperform by more than 10 percentage points. We trace this gap to four patterns prevalent across routers: difficulty blindness, length reversal, semantic matching, and roster suboptimality. We show that the first three are what the standard objective rewards: cost-accuracy Pareto efficiency on realized costs favors escalating moderately hard queries over the hardest ones, shorter queries over longer ones, and routing by a query's source over its difficulty. We also argue that the two assumptions that would justify large rosters, model granularity and model specialization, do not hold empirically. We propose an alternative evaluation methodology that does not reward these patterns, and as a proof of concept, we design a simple two-model router that avoids all four. Nevertheless, its gain over random routing is limited, because a well-chosen roster leaves little to route.

</details>


### [211] [Law And Order: Tax Law Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02792)
*Sophia Simeng Han,Yoshiki Takashima,Anjiang Wei,Zhaoyu Li,Michael Genesereth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Law&Order神经符号框架，将税法表格与说明自动形式化为可执行符号程序，在51份留出报税单上达到100%单元级与表单级准确率，远超最优LLM的66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律系统日益依赖软件实现，但将法律文本可扩展地翻译为准确符号表示的方法仍不成熟。税法中的表格与填报说明包含算术、分支、递归和表格推理构成的大型计算结构，是研究该问题的合适场景。

Method: 框架建立两种法律与逻辑的对应关系：结构对应（对齐单元格、附表等法律与符号组件）与指称对应（要求符号组件实现其法律对应物所规定的计算）。方法结合大语言模型合成、单元级验证，并利用人工编写的OpenTaxSolver报税单进行迭代式局部错误修复。

Result: 在独立编写、生成与修复阶段从未接触的留出TaxCalcBench报税单上评估：最先进LLM仅66%准确率，而Law&Order在51份留出报税单上实现100%单元级与表单级准确率。

Conclusion: 将基于LLM的合成与符号验证相结合，相比单独使用LLM，能有效实现可扩展且可验证的大规模法律自动形式化。

Abstract: Legal systems are increasingly implemented through software, yet scalable methods for translating legal texts into accurate symbolic representations remain underdeveloped. We study this problem through tax law, where forms and filing instructions define large computational structures involving arithmetic, branching, recursion, and tabular reasoning. We propose Law&Order, a neuro-symbolic framework for automatically formalizing tax forms and instructions into executable symbolic programs. Our approach establishes two forms of correspondence between law and logic: structural correspondence, which aligns legal and symbolic components such as cells and schedules, and denotational correspondence, which requires symbolic components to implement the computations specified by their legal counterparts. We combine large language model synthesis with cell-level verification and iterative localized error repair using human-written OpenTaxSolver tax returns. We then evaluate the resulting formalizations on independently authored, held-out TaxCalcBench returns, that are never exposed during generation or repair. Although the most advanced LLM achieves only 66% accuracy, Law&Order achieves 100% cell-level and form-level accuracy on 51 held-out returns, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining LLM-based synthesis with symbolic verification for scalable and verifiable large-scale legal autoformalization compared with using an LLM alone.

</details>


### [212] [PAPER2LLM++: Continual Self-Evolution of LLMs from Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02793)
*Hongji Pu,Yilun Zhao,Wenpeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAPER2LLM++ 框架，使 LLM 能够从研究论文中持续自我进化：将论文视为模型改进的证据与监督信号，而非仅用于检索的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过研究不断发现 LLM 的局限、成因与解决方案，但这些发现与模型自身演化基本脱节——模型无法自动从关于自身失败的新研究中学习。因此需要打通“人类发现”与“模型进化”之间的闭环。

Method: PAPER2LLM++ 面向逐篇到来的论文流：1）抽取有证据支撑的发现（evidence-grounded findings）；2）检验该论文所报告的局限在当前模型中是否仍然存在；3）若存在，则将发现转化为候选学习信号；4）通过 try-evaluate-commit 流程，仅当更新能改善目标行为、且不显著遗忘既有改进、不损害通用能力时，才将其合并入模型。

Result: 在由研究发现的 LLM 失败构成的顺序论文流上，模型能够逐步吸收新发现，同时保留此前的改进效果。

Conclusion: PAPER2LLM++ 朝闭合人类发现与模型进化之间的环路迈出了一步，使模型能够从关于自身局限与改进的研究中持续学习。

Abstract: Research on LLMs continually uncovers model limitations, their causes, and potential solutions. Yet these human discoveries remain largely disconnected from model evolution: an LLM does not automatically learn from new research about its own failures. We introduce PAPER2LLM++, a framework for continual self-evolution of LLMs from research papers. Rather than treating papers merely as knowledge to retrieve, PAPER2LLM++ uses the growing literature as a stream of evidence and supervision for model improvement. For each incoming paper, it extracts evidence-grounded findings, tests whether the reported limitation persists in the current model, and, when needed, converts the findings into candidate learning signals. A try-evaluate-commit procedure integrates an update only when it improves the targeted behavior without substantially forgetting prior improvements or degrading general capabilities. Across a sequential stream of research-discovered LLM failures, we show that models can progressively incorporate new findings while retaining earlier gains. PAPER2LLM++ thus takes a step toward closing the loop between human discovery and model evolution, enabling models to continually learn from research about their own limitations and improvements.

</details>


### [213] [Modeling Shared and Individual Structure for Cross-Subject Continuous Affect Regression from EEG-fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02796)
*Xuan Wang,Bing Wang,Shuai Chang,Hao Yuan,Xinbo Qi,Xinyue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究零样本跨被试的连续效价-唤醒度估计：在同步EEG-fNIRS数据上，仅凭测试被试的无标签信号预测[1,255]原始尺度情感轨迹，通过alpha波段跨通道同步性进行个体化增益校准，MAE显著优于EEGNet和ASAC-Net基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续情感（效价-唤醒度）回归多在依赖被试（subject-dependent）设定下研究，模型可看到同一被试的标注数据；而零样本跨被试设定更困难且更贴近实际部署需求，即模型需对从未见过标签的被试进行预测。作者希望在无任何测试被试标签的前提下，从生理信号直接预测原始尺度的情感轨迹。

Method: 在同步EEG-fNIRS数据集上，将情感轨迹分解为两部分：(1) 观看相同视频刺激的被试间共享结构；(2) 由无标签EEG标记（alpha波段跨通道同步性）为每个测试被试估计的个体结构，用以围绕量表中点对共享轨迹进行缩放。验证该个体校准机制的四条独立路径：留一被试交叉验证下标记与真实最优增益的相关性、与非线性替代形式的函数形式比较、重复留4被试的组件消融、以及个体缩放的剩余提升空间上限分析。

Result: 在留出被试上，模型总体MAE为25.96 / 22.80（两个评估批次），其中效价21.94 / 19.6，唤醒29.98 / 26.0，远低于同一被试独立划分下EEGNet（原始尺度得分60.6）和ASAC-Net（55.0）基线。此外系统性的负结果搜索（涵盖模型架构、特征表示和预测目标）未发现任何能超越单一alpha同步性标记的信号。

Conclusion: 单一alpha波段跨通道同步性标记结合共享-个体轨迹分解，可在零样本跨被试设定下有效实现原始尺度的连续效价-唤醒度估计，性能大幅超越既有基线；同时负结果搜索表明该标记具有不可替代性，个体缩放仍是剩余误差的主要可控来源。

Abstract: Continuous, second-by-second valence-arousal estimation from physiological signals is typically studied in a subject-dependent setting, where the model sees labeled data from the same person it is later evaluated on. We study the harder zero-shot cross-subject variant on a synchronized EEG-fNIRS dataset: predict raw-scale ([1, 255]) valence and arousal trajectories for subjects whose labels the model never observes, given only their unlabeled EEG/fNIRS recordings while watching the same video stimuli as a disjoint set of training subjects. We decompose the affect trajectory into a structure shared across subjects who watch the same stimuli and an individual structure estimated for each test subject from a label-free EEG marker (alpha-band cross-channel synchrony), which rescales the shared trajectory around the scale midpoint. We validate the per-subject calibration mechanism on four independent axes: leave-one-subject-out correlation between the marker and each subject's true optimal gain, a functional-form comparison against non-linear alternatives, a repeated leave-4-out component ablation isolating each part of the pipeline's contribution, and a ceiling analysis bounding the remaining headroom for per-subject scaling. On held-out subjects, the model reaches an overall MAE of 25.96 / 22.80 across two evaluation batches (valence 21.94 / 19.6, arousal 29.98 / 26.0), well below EEGNet and ASAC-Net baselines reported for the same subject-independent split (raw scale score 60.6 and 55.0 respectively). We further report a systematic negative-result search across model architectures, feature representations, and prediction targets that found no signal able to improve on the single alpha-synchrony marker.

</details>


### [214] [BitNest: Bit-Nested Speculative Decoding for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02800)
*Chence Yang,Ningxi Cheng,Arash Akbari,Qitao Tan,Qingchan Zhu,Ci Zhang,Changdi Yang,Yanzhi Wang,Wei Niu,Jinhui Wang,Jin Lu,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitNest提出一种位嵌套式自推测解码框架，将低精度草稿模型嵌入高精度目标模型的权重表示中，两者共享同一物理权重，并扩展到KV缓存，在保持高精度模型质量的同时实现1.48-1.61倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法通常需要额外的草稿模型或权重表示，在资源受限设备上带来不可忽略的内存开销；自推测方法虽能降低开销，但在草稿质量、目标质量和存储效率之间仍存在权衡。因此需要一种能在不增加额外存储的前提下兼顾草稿质量与目标质量的方案。

Method: BitNest采用位嵌套（bit-nested）设计：不从预定义的高精度目标模型派生草稿，而是先构建一个强的低精度基础模型，再通过残差精炼恢复高精度目标，使草稿与目标共享单一物理权重表示。该渐进精度设计进一步扩展到KV缓存，以支持长上下文推理。

Result: 在多个7B-8B边缘友好型LLM和多样化工作负载上，BitNest实现平均95.2%的推测接受率，且较好地保持高精度模型质量，相比FP16自回归解码获得1.48-1.61倍端到端加速。在所有代表性自推测基线均支持的LLaMA模型上，BitNest也取得持续具有竞争力或更高的解码加速。

Conclusion: BitNest通过将低精度草稿嵌入高精度目标权重表示并扩展至KV缓存，在不引入额外存储开销的情况下实现了高质量草稿与高接受率的推测解码，为资源受限设备上的高效LLM推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft to propose multiple tokens for parallel verification. However, existing methods often require an additional draft model or weight representation, introducing non-negligible memory overhead on resource-constrained devices. Self-speculative approaches reduce this overhead, yet still face trade-offs between draft quality, target quality, and storage efficiency. We propose BitNest, a bit-nested speculative decoding framework that embeds a low-precision draft directly into the higher-precision target representation. Instead of deriving a draft from a predefined target, BitNest first constructs a strong low-precision base and then recovers the higher-precision target through residual refinement, enabling both models to share a single physical weight representation. BitNest further extends this progressive-precision design to the KV cache for long-context inference. Across multiple 7B--8B edge-friendly LLMs and diverse workloads, BitNest achieves an average speculative acceptance rate of 95.2% while closely preserving higher-precision model quality, and delivers 1.48--1.61x end-to-end speedup over FP16 autoregressive decoding. On the LLaMA models supported by all representative self-speculative baselines, BitNest also achieves consistently competitive or higher decoding speedup.

</details>


### [215] [VIGOR: Zero-Shot Visual Generalization via Latent-Space Consistency in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02801)
*Mingyu Park,Samyeul Noh,Hyun Myung,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIGOR通过在隐空间施加弱-强增强一致性与动力学一致性，解决基于模型RL在视觉干扰下的两级脆弱性，实现零样本视觉泛化并保持样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）虽在学得隐动力学中规划而样本效率高，但在未见视觉干扰（背景变化、光照变化、相机位移）下性能显著下降。与无模型RL中编码器扰动只影响单步预测不同，MBRL存在两级脆弱性：视觉干扰先使编码器输出偏离分布，误差再经规划视界内的递归隐状态推演而累积放大。

Method: 提出VIGOR框架，包含三个相互依赖的组件：（i）非对称弱到强增强，在同一批次内配对仅弱增强与弱到强增强的隐视图；（ii）动力学级一致性，通过直接隐回归强制增强不变的转移预测；（iii）编码器级稳定，防止在动力学级一致性所施加的跨增强监督下编码器漂移。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite（DMC）与Robosuite上，VIGOR优于最先进的无模型与基于模型基线，在DMC上超次优基线3.4%，在Robosuite上超43.6%。消融显示鲁棒性与增强方式无关：替换为不同扰动族的替代增强仍保持强泛化，证实驱动鲁棒性的是隐空间一致性而非增强选择。

Conclusion: 隐空间一致性是MBRL零样本视觉泛化的关键，VIGOR在保留MBRL骨干样本效率的同时显著提升对未见视觉干扰的鲁棒性，且该鲁棒性不依赖于特定增强方法。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) achieves strong sample efficiency by planning within learned latent dynamics, yet its performance degrades substantially under unseen visual distractions such as background variations, lighting changes, or camera shifts. Unlike model-free RL, where encoder perturbations affect only single-step predictions, MBRL suffers from a two-level vulnerability: visual distractions first push encoder outputs out of distribution, and these errors then compound through recursive latent rollouts over the planning horizon. We propose visual generalization via latent-space consistency in model-based RL (VIGOR), a framework that enables zero-shot generalization to unseen visual distractions while retaining the sample efficiency of its MBRL backbone. VIGOR integrates three interdependent components: (i) asymmetric weak-to-strong augmentation, which pairs weak-only and weak-to-strong latent views within a single batch; (ii) dynamics-level consistency, which enforces augmentation-invariant transition predictions through direct latent regression; and (iii) encoder-level stabilization, which prevents encoder drift under the cross-augmentation supervision imposed by dynamics-level consistency. Evaluations on the DeepMind Control Suite (DMC) and Robosuite show that VIGOR outperforms state-of-the-art model-free and model-based baselines, surpassing the second-best baseline by 3.4% on DMC and 43.6% on Robosuite. Ablations further show that VIGOR's robustness is augmentation-agnostic: replacing the default augmentation with alternatives from distinct perturbation families preserves strong generalization, confirming that latent-space consistency, not the augmentation choice, drives robustness.

</details>


### [216] [ROUTEAUDIT: Interaction-Aware Identification for Budgeted Multi-Verifier Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02808)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将验证器路由形式化为"契约条件下的识别问题"，提出ROUTEAUDIT协议，通过契约格、策略无关响应带与请求级边界三件可测对象，实现自适应多验证器系统在匹配条件下的公平归因，并给出归因证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应多验证器系统多以端点质量-成本差距进行比较，但验证器目录、可用性、资源核算、信息过滤或评分器会随策略变化，导致比较混淆，无法判断增益究竟来自策略还是验证器集合等坐标变化。因此需要一个在匹配条件下只改变策略坐标的识别框架。

Method: 将验证器路由表述为契约条件下的识别问题：契约记录请求支持、验证器目录、实际可用性、资源核算、在线过滤与轨迹后评分；匹配路由对比仅改变策略坐标。ROUTEAUDIT引入三个可测对象：(1) 契约格，在所有允许的桥接序上平均坐标增量，报告归因及其路径敏感性；(2) 策略无关响应带，用于自适应策略导致观测差异时识别成对序列对比；(3) 针对不完全匹配的请求级边界，利用仍被观测的潜在结果给出锐利的有限总体区间。协议在oracle连接前提交付费观测与账本事件，并为每次比较返回归因证书。

Result: 在两个留出原始尾部缓存上，匹配静态SF+SA等于级联，将相对全静态的0.1797与0.1250表观增益归因于验证器集合优势。在1,319个留出任务请求上，学习策略与RLVR研究分别报告质量0.9522与0.9553，匹配静态为0.9484；RLVR-静态成对差为+0.0068，请求配对区间[0.0015,0.0122]，训练种子×请求分层区间[0.0006,0.0131]。受控归因恢复得到路由平均绝对误差0.0011、端点重构误差0.0004。因子、桥接序与随机提供方研究评估证书接口；RLVR提供同一识别契约下的学习策略压力测试。

Conclusion: 在匹配识别契约下，自适应多验证器的表观增益可被重新归因，部分增益实际源自验证器集合而非策略本身；ROUTEAUDIT通过归因证书与路径敏感性、有限总体区间，提供了可审计、可复现的验证器路由比较方法。

Abstract: Adaptive multi-verifier systems are commonly compared through endpoint quality-cost gaps, even when the verifier catalog, availability, accounting, information filtration, or scorer changes with the policy. We formulate verifier routing as a contract-conditioned identification problem. The contract records request support, verifier catalog, realized availability, resource accounting, online filtration, and post-trace scoring; a matched route contrast changes only the policy coordinate. ROUTEAUDIT adds three measurable objects to this contract. A contract lattice averages coordinate increments over every admissible bridge order and reports the resulting attribution together with its path sensitivity. A policy-independent response tape identifies paired sequential contrasts when adaptive policies reveal different observations. For incomplete matching, request-level bounds use whichever potential outcome remains observed and give a sharp finite-population interval. The protocol commits paid observations and ledger events before the oracle join and returns an attribution certificate for each comparison. On two held-out raw-tail caches, matched static SF+SA equals the cascade, assigning the apparent gains of 0.1797 and 0.1250 over full static to the verifier-set edge. On 1,319 held-out task requests, the learned and RLVR studies report quality 0.9522 and 0.9553 versus 0.9484 for matched static; the RLVR-static paired difference is +0.0068 with a request-paired interval $[0.0015,0.0122]$ and a training-seed-by-request hierarchical interval $[0.0006,0.0131]$. Controlled attribution recovery yields route mean absolute error 0.0011 and endpoint reconstruction error 0.0004. Factorial, bridge-order, and stochastic-provider studies evaluate the certificate interface; RLVR supplies a learned-policy stress test under the same identification contract.

</details>


### [217] [iS-KV: Online Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Block-Incremental SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02815)
*Yiren Zhao,Guanghui Song,Tianrui Qin,Kejiang Ye,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 iS-KV：面向长链推理的在线低秩 KV-cache 压缩方法，通过"近期窗口精确 + 历史增量折叠 + 基更新时同步历史坐标"实现高压缩率下的精度保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维链推理在自回归解码中使 KV-cache 随解码长度线性增长，显存开销巨大。现有 token eviction 方法会不可逆地删除历史状态，而后续推理可能需要回访这些状态；SVD 低秩压缩可保留全部位置但更紧凑，然而从固定 prompt cache 扩展到在线解码存在困难：若为新 token 更新基而旧 token 仍保留旧基下的坐标，会导致历史表示严重漂移。

Method: iS-KV 维护一个近期窗口的精确 KV，并将较旧状态增量折叠进有界秩（bounded-rank）低秩表示；当低秩基随解码演化时，同步更新历史 token 的坐标以与最新基对齐，从而保持表示一致性。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 上，持久 KV 压缩 4.06 倍时准确率 82.6%，接近原始模型的 83.6%；Qwen3-8B 上，5.64 倍压缩时准确率 89.2%。在相同内存预算下，iS-KV 持续优于 token eviction 基线。

Conclusion: iS-KV 通过在线低秩压缩与历史坐标同步机制，在不丢失历史状态的前提下有效控制长链推理的 KV-cache 增长，在多个模型上实现了高压缩率与接近原模型精度的平衡，优于 token eviction 方法。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning substantially increases KV-cache memory during autoregressive decoding, as every generated token introduces new key and value states and causes the cache to grow linearly with decoding length. Existing KV-cache compression methods typically control this growth through token eviction, but irreversible deletion can remove historical states that later reasoning may need to revisit. SVD-based low-rank compression provides an alternative by retaining all positions with a more compact representation. However, extending it from a fixed prompt cache to online decoding is non-trivial. Through our investigation, we find that if the basis is updated for new tokens while old tokens keep their coordinates in the old basis, the stored history drifts substantially. Based on this observation, we propose iS-KV, an online low-rank KV-cache compression method for long-horizon reasoning. iS-KV keeps a recent window exact while incrementally folding older states into bounded-rank representations. As the low-rank basis evolves, it synchronizes historical coordinates with the updated basis to maintain representation consistency. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, iS-KV achieves 82.6% accuracy at 4.06-fold persistent-KV compression, close to the original model's 83.6%. On Qwen3-8B, it achieves 89.2% accuracy at 5.64-fold compression. Under matched memory budgets, iS-KV consistently outperforms token-eviction baselines.

</details>


### [218] [MetaRubric: Learning to Reward for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02824)
*Yuxuan Fan,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MetaRubric，通过证据感知的策略优化与响应引导的评分标准自适应交替进行，解决 rubric 强化学习中的"空洞得分"问题，在多个医学基准上显著优于静态评判的 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准（rubric）的强化学习将奖励驱动优化扩展到开放式任务，通过对各项响应要求给予部分分数来实现。然而，评分评判器可能在响应中缺少某条准则所要求的信息或动作时仍给出高分，作者将这一失效模式称为"空洞得分"（Vacuous Credit）。这种给分即使在删除所需信息后依然存在，并且可能反转某条响应 GRPO 优势值的符号，从而误导优化。

Method: 提出 MetaRubric，交替进行两个阶段：（1）证据感知的策略优化——通过在每个提示中改变一个与任务相关的事实来构造反事实对照，只有当响应包含足以满足所需 rubric 准则的证据时才分配得分；（2）响应引导的评分标准自适应——在每个策略优化阶段后，用当前策略的响应指导对原始与反事实准则的修订，同时保持原始提示初始 rubric 在各提示事实下解释的语义不变。此外，在阶段边界自适应调整准则权重，以更好地针对观察到的策略错误。

Result: 在多种骨干模型上，MetaRubric 相较静态评判的 GRPO 将 PubMedQA 准确率提升 6.00–20.40 个百分点，并在 HealthBench-Hard 及两个多模态医学基准上取得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 证据感知的得分与反事实构造能有效抑制空洞得分，配合响应引导的 rubric 自适应与准则权重调整，可显著提升开放式医学任务的强化学习效果，并在多个骨干与基准上验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning extends reward-driven optimization to open-ended tasks by assigning partial credit to individual response requirements. However, rubric judges can assign a high criterion score even when the information or action it requires is absent from the response, a failure mode we term Vacuous Credit. Such awards persist after the required information is removed and can reverse the sign of a response's GRPO advantage. To address this problem, we introduce MetaRubric, which alternates evidence-aware policy optimization with response-guided rubric adaptation. We construct counterfactual counterparts by changing one task-relevant fact in each prompt. During policy optimization, credit is assigned only when the response contains sufficient evidence to satisfy the required rubric criterion. After each policy-optimization stage, current policy responses guide revisions to original and counterfactual criteria while preserving the meaning of the original prompt's initial rubric as interpreted under each prompt's facts. We also adapt criterion weights at stage boundaries to better address observed policy errors. Across multiple backbones, MetaRubric improves PubMedQA accuracy by 6.00--20.40 percentage points over static-judge GRPO, with further gains on HealthBench-Hard and two multimodal medical benchmarks.

</details>


### [219] [Scaling Trajectories for Complex Tasks through Recursive Self-Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02826)
*Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Chengsong Huang,Fuxiao Liu,Haitao Mi,Jordan Boyd-Graber,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出递归自我重写（RSR）框架，用同一基座模型在多种专用harness下发现成功解，并重写为通用harness下可用的训练轨迹，用于监督微调，显著提升终端任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用harness（如各类外部工具/环境封装）虽能在困难任务上产生成功轨迹，但其干预在部署时往往不可用。因此需要一个框架，将"多样harness辅助下获得的经验"重构为"通用harness下可复用的能力"。

Method: RSR框架包含三个角色：(1) Planner：将成功解中的过程抽取为runbook；(2) Critic：筛查验证器与解泄漏，并指导递归修订；(3) Executor：在全新沙箱中遵循合格runbook执行，从而在通用harness下重建轨迹。整个流程基于单一基座模型Qwen-3.8-27B，在约3K自采集终端任务上运行多种harness。

Result: 三种harness联合解决759个任务，比记录池中最强单一harness多34.3%。RSR将2,001条成功源轨迹扩展为11,094条重写轨迹用于SFT。相比基座模型：Terminal-Bench 2的pass@3从57.0%升至74.2%；Terminal-Bench 4从1.5%升至9.1%；自采集Terminal-Bench Hard从39.0%升至63.0%；Software Terminal-Bench从3.0%升至6.0%；Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench的过程奖励从0.21升至0.29。训练后性能亦优于直接轨迹SFT。

Conclusion: 多样harness辅助下的经验可被重构为通用harness下可复用的能力，说明通过harness发现与递归重写，无需部署时的专用干预即可提升模型在长程终端任务上的表现。

Abstract: Successful trajectories on difficult tasks provide valuable supervision for model improvement, but specialized harnesses introduce interventions that may be unavailable during deployment. We propose Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR), a framework that uses one base model, Qwen-3.8-27B, to discover successful solutions under diverse harnesses and reconstruct them as training trajectories under a general harness. A planner extracts procedures into runbooks, a critic screens for verifier and solution leakage and guides recursive revision, and an executor follows qualified runbooks in fresh sandboxes. Across approximately 3K self-curated terminal tasks, three harnesses jointly solve 759 tasks, 34.3% more than the strongest individual harness in the recorded pool. RSR expands 2,001 successful source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten trajectories for supervised finetuning. Training on these trajectories outperforms both the base model and direct trajectory SFT. Compared with the base model, pass@3 increases from 57.0% to 74.2% on Terminal-Bench 2, from 1.5% to 9.1% on Terminal-Bench 4, from 39.0% to 63.0% on our self-curated Terminal-Bench Hard, and from 3.0% to 6.0% on our Software Terminal-Bench. Process reward on Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench rises from 0.21 to 0.29. These results show how diverse harness-assisted experiences can be reconstructed into reusable capabilities for a model operating under a general harness.

</details>


### [220] [MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02827)
*Carsten Maple,Cagatay Yucel,Isaac Holeman,Chris Knotz,Peter Mattson,James Goel,Jonathan Petit,Sean McGregor,James Ezick,Abhishek Kumar,Alicia Parrish,Murali Emani,Kashyap Iyer,Faiza Khan Khattak,Washington Mbonu,Daniel Machlab,Eileen Long,Shaona Ghosh,Jibin Varghese,Roman Lutz,Andrew Gruen,Bennett Hillenbrand,Prabal Gupta,Mohammed Serrhini,Dhivya Nagasubramanian,Aakash Gupta,Jun,Lu,Kurt Bollacker,Chang Liu,Jonathan Petit,Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Brent Miller,Apurv Verma,Roman Eng,Armstrong Foundjem,Mohammed Serrhini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLCommons 发布 Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0，提出端到端的单轮文本越狱评测方法，并在 8 个开源权重模型上测得平均 Resilience Gap 为 7.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 系统设有拒绝危险请求的安全机制，而越狱提示可绕过这些防护并诱导其输出本应拒绝的内容。领域内缺乏统一、可复现的越狱鲁棒性评测流程，因此需要建立标准化基准以支持系统间的可比评估。

Method: 该基准整合标准驱动的系统与攻击选择、基线与对抗配对评测、人工标注、自动评估器校准、评分与分级，以及风险校准的信息披露。评测使用 264 条种子提示，覆盖 11 个危害类别，并从 MLCommons Jailbreak Taxonomy 中选取代表性攻击，面向 8 个开源权重系统；响应依据 AILuminate Assessment Standard v1.4 评估，以 Resilience Gap（基线与对抗条件下安全性能之差）衡量鲁棒性。

Result: 全部被评系统与攻击下，不安全响应率由基线的 11.08% 升至越狱条件下的 18.65%，平均 Resilience Gap 为 7.57%。可访问性较高的系统平均差距更大；攻击有效性在不同攻击类别与危害类型间差异显著。研究还考察了评估器可靠性及测量误差来源。

Conclusion: Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 不仅给出评测结果，更建立了可复现的比较性越狱评测方法学基础，为未来在系统、攻击、危害与评估方法维度的扩展提供支撑。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are designed to refuse hazardous requests. A jailbreak is a prompt crafted to bypass those safeguards and elicit outputs that the system would normally refuse to provide. The MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 provides an end-to-end methodology for evaluating the robustness of large language models to single-turn, text-based jailbreak attacks. It combines criteria-driven system and attack selection, paired baseline and adversarial evaluation, human annotation, automated evaluator calibration, scoring, grading, and risk-calibrated disclosure within a single benchmarking pipeline. The benchmark evaluates eight open-weight systems using 264 seed prompts spanning eleven hazard categories and representative attacks drawn from the MLCommons Jailbreak Taxonomy. Responses are assessed using the AILuminate Assessment Standard v1.4, and robustness is measured through the Resilience Gap: the change in safety performance between baseline and adversarial conditions. Across all evaluated systems and attacks, the unsafe-response rate increased from 11.08% under baseline conditions to 18.65% under jailbreak conditions, producing an average Resilience Gap of 7.57%. Accessible systems showed a larger mean gap, while attack effectiveness varied substantially across attack categories and hazards. The benchmark also examines evaluator reliability and sources of measurement error. Beyond reporting results, Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 establishes a reproducible methodological foundation for comparative jailbreak evaluation and for future expansion across systems, attacks, hazards, and evaluation methods.

</details>


### [221] [FSPO: Policy-Consistent Risk and Pareto-Feasible Control for Budgeted LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02828)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FSPO 反馈状态控制器，联合解决预算受限 LLM RL 后训练中的风险估计失配、校准漂移与多资源可行性认证问题，在匹配 GRPO 资源下超越最强自适应基线 PB2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应 LLM 强化学习后训练会在线调整多个训练执行器（rollout 温度、组大小、裁剪、KL 正则、验证器分配、更新预算），但存在三个耦合未解问题：由行为轨迹训练的未来风险模型未必能估计将部署控制器的风险；在已记录状态-动作对上校准的分数在选择性动作选择后会失准；各资源独立最小成本通常无法证明多资源可行延续。

Method: FSPO 是一个用于预算受限 LLM RL 后训练的反馈状态控制器，联合处理上述问题。其学习一个策略一致的风险-to-go 模型（Bellman 目标与用于未来决策的同一冻结控制器一致）及长时程效用模型；提出决策条件轨迹校准（DCTC），在由临时控制器所选动作生成的交叉拟合轨迹上校准风险；并设计 Pareto 资源延续证书（PRCC），仅当非支配累积预留量在剩余时域内可行时才接受动作。

Result: 在匹配 GRPO 资源包络下，FSPO 达到 66.11% 留出集与 59.43% OOD 准确率，优于最强自适应基线 PB2 的 64.47% 与 57.03%。三个配对训练种子相对上下文赌博机在留出集与 OOD 上分别提升 +2.42 与 +3.19 个百分点。高行为-部署失配下，策略一致风险将选中决策 ECE 从 0.108 降至 0.053；DCTC 在匹配接受率下将其从 0.039 降至 0.022；PRCC 在 18 动作目录上消除假可行准入（$0.197→0.000$）；因子消融中启用全部三组件将轨迹失败率从 0.181 降至 0.083。

Conclusion: FSPO 通过策略一致风险建模、DCTC 与 PRCC 的联合设计，在资源受限的 LLM RL 后训练中同时改善准确率、风险校准与多资源可行性认证，并显著降低轨迹失败率；消融实验证实三组件各自及协同贡献。

Abstract: Adaptive LLM reinforcement-learning post-training changes multiple training actuators online, including rollout temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, and update budget. Three coupled issues remain unresolved. A future-risk model trained from behavior trajectories need not estimate the risk induced by the controller that will be deployed; a score calibrated on logged state-action pairs can become miscalibrated after selective action choice; and independent per-resource minimum costs do not in general certify a feasible multi-resource continuation. We introduce FSPO, a feedback-state controller for budgeted LLM RL post-training that addresses these issues jointly. FSPO learns a policy-consistent risk-to-go model whose Bellman target follows the same frozen controller used for future decisions, together with a long-horizon utility model. Decision-conditioned trajectory calibration (DCTC) calibrates risk on cross-fitted trajectories generated by actions selected by provisional controllers. A Pareto resource continuation certificate (PRCC) admits an action only when a non-dominated cumulative reservation remains feasible over the residual horizon. Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO reaches 66.11% held-out and 59.43% OOD accuracy, compared with 64.47% and 57.03% for PB2, the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline. Three paired training seeds give gains of +2.42 and +3.19 percentage points over the contextual bandit on held-out and OOD evaluation. Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.

</details>


### [222] [AMBER: Multi-View Adaptive Budget Allocation for Listwise Vision-Language Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02831)
*Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Congmin Zheng,Teng Wang,Junjie Wu,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMBER，一种在线预算化多视图重排序框架，通过连续Elo更新与两级计算分配，在有限VLM调用预算下实现全局最优资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）作为多模态检索的listwise重排序器性能强大，但推理成本高，只能评估少量局部候选视图。现有多调用策略依赖固定调度，将昂贵的VLM调用浪费在无信息量的候选对和简单查询上。

Method: AMBER将碎片化的listwise VLM输出视为局部锦标赛，用连续Elo更新维护轻量级全局排序状态。在此基础上进行两级计算分配：一是动态构建高分歧义性的候选视图，二是调度查询以最大化期望信息增益。理论上证明每次Elo更新对应Bradley-Terry对数似然上的随机梯度上升步，并为查询级分配策略提供子模信息论动机。

Result: 在CIRR、CIRCO和PhotoBench上的实验表明，在可比的VLM调用预算下，AMBER在所比较的多调用VLM重排序方法中取得最强的整体性能，且在更低预算设置下仍然有效。

Conclusion: AMBER通过在线预算化的多视图重排序与动态全局资源分配，有效提升了多模态检索中VLM重排序的预算效率与整体性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful listwise rerankers for multimodal retrieval, but high inference costs restrict them to evaluating small local candidate views. Existing multi-call strategies rely on fixed schedules, wasting expensive VLM calls on uninformative candidate pairs and easy queries. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-view Budgeted Elo Reranking (AMBER), an online, budgeted multi-view reranking framework that dynamically optimizes global resource allocation. AMBER treats fragmented listwise VLM outputs as local tournaments, using continuous Elo updates to maintain a lightweight global ranking state. Building on this, it allocates computation at two levels: dynamically constructing candidate views with high score ambiguity, and scheduling queries to maximize expected information gain. We show that each Elo update corresponds to a stochastic gradient ascent step on the Bradley-Terry log-likelihood, and provide a submodular information-theoretic motivation for the query-level allocation strategy. Experiments on CIRR, CIRCO, and PhotoBench demonstrate that AMBER achieves the strongest overall performance among the compared multi-call VLM reranking methods under comparable VLM-call budgets, while remaining effective in lower-budget settings. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wnlfc/AMBER.

</details>


### [223] [DNAlign: Dynamic Null-Space Safe Alignment for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02844)
*Jisheng Dang,Yushuo Zhao,Dewei Liu,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Tiantian Rao,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DNAlign：一种结合控制论优化与零空间投影的轻量级对齐框架，在降低有害输出的同时保持流畅性与事实效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐方法要么计算开销高，要么会误伤模型核心知识，导致良性任务上的流畅度与事实准确性下降，暴露出安全性与效用之间的持续权衡问题。

Method: 将LLM视为动态系统，引入可控扰动以引导生成走向安全行为；核心是投影模块，将扰动限制在由中性隐状态导出的有害相关子空间中，从而保留通用知识与响应质量；并基于人类偏好数据训练价值函数，自适应优化控制信号以贴合人类安全偏好。

Result: 在多种LLM主干上的广泛评估表明，该框架持续减少有害输出，同时保持流畅性、连贯性与事实效用；相比既有对齐基线取得更优的整体表现，且不牺牲生成多样性。

Conclusion: 该框架为安全的LLM对齐提供了一种有效且可实际部署的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe and reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) remains a fundamental challenge. Existing safety alignment approaches either incur high computational cost or unintentionally disrupt the model's core knowledge, leading to degraded fluency and factual accuracy on benign tasks. This reveals a persistent trade-off between safety and utility. We propose DNAlign, a lightweight alignment framework that integrates control-theoretic optimization with null-space projection. By treating the LLM as a dynamic system, the proposed framework introduces controllable perturbations to steer generation toward safe behavior. A key component is the projection module, which restricts these perturbations to the harmful-related subspace derived from neutral hidden states, thereby preserving general knowledge and response quality. A value function trained on human preference data adaptively optimizes the control signals to align with human safety preferences. Extensive evaluations across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that our framework consistently reduces harmful outputs while maintaining fluency, coherence, and factual utility. It achieves superior overall performance compared to prior alignment baselines without sacrificing generation diversity. These results indicate that the proposed framework provides an effective and practically deployable solution for safe LLM alignment. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DNAlign.

</details>


### [224] [Bounded Reachability & Jailbreak Detection via Contraction-Constrained State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02853)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为基于状态空间模型（SSM）的安全头建立了形式化鲁棒性认证理论：当状态转移矩阵满足 l∞ 范数小于 1 的收缩条件时，可对线性时不变分类器进行精确区间界传播（IBP）认证；违反该条件则区间随序列长度指数增长，认证不可行。实验在有毒评论数据上将认证比例从 41% 提升至 59%，并在越狱检测任务上实现零样本迁移（AdvBench DR=0.994，HarmBench DR=0.988）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全头（safety head）作为附加在预训练语言模型上的轻量分类器，其经验检测性能已有研究，但形式化鲁棒性属性几乎未被探索。作者希望回答：SSM 安全头在何种条件下能被认证为对嵌入空间有界扰动内的所有输入给出相同预测。

Method: 作者针对线性时不变（LTI）分类器进行理论分析，证明认证能力取决于状态转移矩阵的 l∞ 范数是否小于 1（收缩条件）。满足时可得到有界稳态宽度的可达输出区间并完成精确 IBP 认证；不满足时区间随序列长度指数增长。作者用铰链惩罚（hinge penalty）强制收缩，并在有毒评论数据与 JailbreakBench 上验证，同时将收缩正则化的 S4 头与基于 Mamba-130M 均值池化嵌入的逻辑回归进行对比。

Result: 在有毒评论数据上，认证比例从 41% 提升到 59%，且在 ‖A‖∞=1 处出现与理论一致的尖锐经验相变。收缩正则化 S4 头在 JailbreakBench 越狱检测上实现零样本迁移，AdvBench 上 DR=0.994，HarmBench 上 DR=0.988。基于 Mamba-130M 均值池化嵌入的逻辑回归在所有检测指标上持平或超过 S4 头。

Conclusion: S4 安全头的价值不在于更强的判别能力，而在于提供了任何基于探针（probe）的方法都无法给出的形式化认证保证。同时，逻辑回归结果说明有害意图在嵌入空间中已近似线性可分。

Abstract: Safety heads are lightweight classifiers attached to pretrained language models for flagging harmful inputs before generation. Their empirical detection performance has been studied, but their formal robustness properties remain largely unexplored. We ask when a State Space Model (SSM)-based safety head can be certified to produce the same prediction for all inputs within a bounded embedding-space perturbation. We prove that the answer turns on a single condition: the $l_\infty$ norm of the state transition matrix must satisfy $\norm{A}_\infty<1$ (the \emph{contraction condition}), which enables exact interval bound propagation (IBP) certification for linear time-invariant classifiers. When the contraction condition holds, the reachable output interval has bounded steady-state width and examples can be certified as robustly classified. When it fails, the interval grows exponentially with sequence length and certification is impossible at any practical perturbation radius. We enforce contraction with a hinge penalty and show on toxic comment data that certified fraction improves from 41\% to 59\%, with a sharp empirical phase transition at $\norm{A}_\infty=1$ matching the theory. Applying a contraction-regularized S4 head to jailbreak detection on JailbreakBench, we achieve a zero-shot transfer to AdvBench (DR=0.994) and HarmBench (DR=0.988). A logistic regression on mean-pooled Mamba-130M embeddings matches or exceeds the S4 head on every detection metric, confirming that harmful intent is already linearly separable in the embedding space. The S4 safety head's contribution is not superior discrimination but the formal certification that no probe-based approach provides.

</details>


### [225] [Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858)
*Moonseok Choi,Taehong Moon,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD)，将蒸馏重点放在教师模型超越 harness 所提供的部分，通过动作偏好对比与有效性检查两个组件，在无需任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息的情况下，在多组长时程智能体基准上优于同固定 harness 的在线策略蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体通常部署在 harness（管理上下文、工具与反馈的软件环境）中。将智能体蒸馏到更小模型时，harness 保持不变，因此学生主要需要 harness 无法提供的教师特有能её力，例如根据 harness 信息正确行动。然而标准蒸馏模仿教师的完整输出，把 harness 视为输入的一部分，导致学生学到大量 harness 本已提供的信息，而忽略了教师真正增值的部分。

Method: HAD 在在线策略蒸馏基础上补充两个组件：(1) 动作偏好，对比同一教师在有/无 harness 信息时的动作，并在学生自身推理之后进行打分；(2) 有效性检查，丢弃偏好动作与 harness 记录相矛盾的偏好对。该方法无需任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息。

Result: 在多个长时程智能体基准和模型上，HAD 在固定相同 harness 的条件下优于在线策略蒸馏基线。分析表明 HAD 更少进入无效循环，且比基线更常从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: 对比提供了仅靠模仿教师无法获得的信息。HAD 能够自适应地将可学习的反馈保留在权重中，同时从 harness 读取状态信息。

Abstract: Language model agents are deployed with a harness, the software around the model that manages its context, tools, and feedback. When such an agent is distilled into a smaller one, the harness stays in place, so the student mainly needs the teacher-specific abilities that the harness cannot provide, such as acting correctly on harness information. Standard distillation, however, imitates the teacher's full outputs and treats the harness as part of the input. We propose Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD), which focuses distillation on what the teacher adds beyond the harness. HAD complements on-policy distillation with two components: an action preference that contrasts the same teacher's actions with and without the harness information, scored after the student's own reasoning, and a validity check that drops preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts the harness records. We show that the contrast gives the student information that imitating the teacher alone cannot provide, and HAD needs no task rewards, success labels, or future information. Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness. Our analysis shows that HAD enters fewer unproductive loops and recovers from errors more often than the baselines, and suggests that it adaptively keeps learnable feedback in its weights while reading state information from the harness.

</details>


### [226] [TACD: Distilling Efficient Text-to-Motion Models via Terminal Amplification Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02867)
*Wei-Jin Huang,Yuan-Ming Li,Kun-Yu Lin,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yue Yu,Shenghao Ye,Junbin Yuan,Fa-Ting Hong,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACD：一种基于终端放大控制的on-policy蒸馏方法，可将文本到动作的扩散/流教师模型蒸馏为少步高效学生模型，显著降低FID、提升速度并减少显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作模型虽质量与指令跟随性提升，但多步去噪与大模型组件导致部署慢、显存占用高。目标是无需真实动作数据，从文本提示和预训练教师中训练高效生成器。

Method: 提出Terminal-Amplification-Controlled Distillation (TACD)，基于分段on-policy流蒸馏，在student生成轨迹上监督clean-motion预测。识别出在固定监督网格上速度匹配会重复放大去噪端点附近误差的失效模式；TACD将最新的teacher query与student步长绑定，在不改变推理的前提下限制clean-motion空间中的有效损失权重。对扩散教师则采用端点匹配形式。

Result: 在HumanML3D和KIT-ML上改善少步生成：八步HY-Motion学生FID较无界蒸馏降低58%。对扩散教师，四步学生FID低于其50步教师，文本-动作检索相当或更优。在HY-Motion和Kimodo上，八步紧凑组件学生实现7.7-11.9x端到端加速，峰值GPU显存降低3.8-6.7x。

Conclusion: TACD通过将teacher query与student步长绑定，有效抑制端点误差放大，实现高质量少步文本到动作生成，并大幅提升推理效率、降低显存需求，适用于流与扩散两类教师。

Abstract: Recent text-to-motion models have improved motion quality and instruction following, yet many-step denoising and large model components make deployment slow and memory-intensive. We present Terminal-Amplification-Controlled Distillation (TACD), an on-policy approach for training efficient motion generators from text prompts and pretrained teachers, without real-motion training data. Building on segmented on-policy flow distillation, we supervise clean-motion predictions along student-generated trajectories. We identify a failure mode in which velocity matching on a fixed supervision grid repeatedly overweights errors near the denoising endpoint, degrading few-step generation. TACD ties the latest teacher query to the student's step size, bounding the effective loss weights in clean-motion space without changing inference. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT-ML demonstrate improved few-step generation, including a 58% reduction in eight-step HY-Motion student FID relative to distillation without this bound. For diffusion teachers, the endpoint-matching form of TACD yields four-step students with lower FID and matched or improved text-motion retrieval relative to their 50-step teachers on HumanML3D. On HY-Motion and Kimodo, eight-step students with compact components achieve 7.7-11.9x end-to-end speedups and reduce peak GPU memory by 3.8-6.7x relative to their teachers. Project page: https://vkgo.github.io/TACD/

</details>


### [227] [PsyEvo: A Personalized Counseling Agent That Self-Evolves at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02885)
*Yuting Yan,Shihao Xu,Junhao Yu,Mingcong Zuo,Lu Chen,Nan Xiang,Haiyang Geng,Dongjie Tao,Minghao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PsyEvo：一个可在测试时进行来访者个性化与响应策略改进的LLM心理咨询框架，含HBSP、LiPO、SOCA三组件，模拟来访者评测中PsychEval总体分达7.684并优于各消融变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康障碍影响全球大量人群，但受训从业者长期短缺，多数人无法获得充分照护；基于LLM的咨询师有望提供可扩展的对话式心理支持，但仅靠离线训练难以适应个体来访者，也无法在测试时从持续的治疗互动中学习。

Method: 提出PsyEvo框架，实现测试时的来访者特异个性化与响应策略改进，包含三部分：(1) 分层贝叶斯技能策略（HBSP），通过维护由会话反馈更新的每位来访者技能后验，个性化"施加何种干预"；(2) 会话间列表偏好优化（LiPO），利用跨来访者偏好证据更新共享响应适配器，改进"所选技能如何表达"；(3) 状态条件序数信用分配（SOCA），通过一致性校验的比较与序数投影，为上述两组件提供候选偏好与轨迹信用。

Result: 在共享在线队列适应的模拟来访者评测中，PsyEvo于PsychEval取得总体分7.684，并在三次配对运行中均超过所有组件消融变体；在共享配置下移除单个组件会使平均总分下降0.138–0.171，表明各组件在完整框架内具有条件性贡献。代码已开源。

Conclusion: PsyEvo通过分层贝叶斯个性化、跨来访者偏好优化与序数信用分配的结合，使LLM心理咨询在测试时兼具个体适配与策略自我改进能力，消融结果验证了各组件的必要性，为可扩展心理支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect a substantial proportion of the global population, yet a persistent shortage of trained practitioners leaves the majority without adequate care. Large language model (LLM)-based counselors present a promising direction for delivering scalable conversational psychological support. Offline model training alone leaves limited room to adapt to individual clients or to learn from ongoing therapeutic interaction at test time. We introduce PsyEvo, an LLM-based counseling framework that enables both client-specific personalization and response-policy improvement at test time through three components: Hierarchical Bayesian Skill Policy (HBSP) personalizes what intervention to apply by maintaining a per-client skill posterior updated from session feedback; Inter-session Listwise Preference Optimization (LiPO) improves how the selected skill is expressed by updating a shared response adapter from cross-client preference evidence; and State-conditioned Ordinal Credit Assignment (SOCA) supplies candidate preferences and trajectory credit to the two components through consistency-checked comparisons and ordinal projection. In simulated-client evaluation with shared online cohort adaptation, PsyEvo obtains 7.684 Overall on PsychEval and exceeds every component variant in each of three matched runs. Removing individual components lowers mean overall score by 0.138--0.171 under the shared configuration, supporting conditional contributions within the complete scaffold. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/PsyEvo

</details>


### [228] [Interpreting at Write Time: A Policy Ablation for Multi-Goal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02897)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究长时助手记忆的摘要策略：无目标摘要、全目标摘要、逐目标摘要三种写入策略对比，逐目标摘要最优，全目标摘要最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时助手无法保留全部历史，必须压缩为摘要，而摘要并非中立：保留什么取决于"记录为何而存"这一目标预设，且该选择在用户后续需求出现之前就已一次性做出。当历史过长、摘要取代原始流后，被省略的信息即永久丢失。因此需要研究：当记忆同时服务于多个长期目标时，摘要应为何种目标服务。

Method: 提出三种写入策略并加以对比：(1) 不设目标的中立摘要；(2) 覆盖所有目标的单一摘要；(3) 每个目标各写一份摘要、读取时合并。在多个模型与事件流上进行实验，固定读取步骤，仅改变写入方式，以隔离写入策略的影响。

Result: 目标确实会使摘要分化：不同目标的摘要彼此重叠度低于某摘要与其自身重写版本的相似度。逐目标摘要在相关性、完整性和准确性上均最优；全目标摘要即使预算远超中立摘要，表现仍不如后者。

Conclusion: 在写入阶段进行目标解释是有收益的，但收益仅体现在后续真正提问的那个目标上。

Abstract: A long-running assistant cannot keep everything it has seen, so it summarises. Summarising is not neutral: what is kept is chosen against some notion of what the record is for, and that choice is made once, before anyone knows which of the user's standing goals will ask. Goals rarely disagree about what happened. They disagree about which parts of it were worth the space. Once the history is too long to re-read, the summary replaces the stream, and whatever it left out is gone. We ask what a memory should summarise for when it serves several standing goals at once. Three policies answer differently: summarise with no goal in view, write one summary covering every goal, or write one summary per goal and read them together. We compare them across several models and event streams, holding the read step fixed so that only the write differs. The goals do pull apart: summaries written for different goals overlap each other less than a summary overlaps a rewrite of itself. Per-goal summaries win on relevance, completeness and accuracy, and the all-goal summary loses even to the neutral one written at a fraction of its budget. Interpreting at write pays off, but only for the goal that later asks.

</details>


### [229] [LUMOS: Tracing Parametric Knowledge from Training Data to Behavioral Outputs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02902)
*Seoyeon Ye,Gayoung Kim,Jiyoung Hong,Sookyung Kim,Hyunsoo Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LUMOS诊断框架，沿"训练数据暴露→行为输出"因果链追踪LLM知识，揭示模型内部编码与行为表达间的检索鸿沟及自我反思校准缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM参数化知识的分析多为输出导向，仅从模型回答推断其"知道什么"，却未验证模型实际训练内容。这使"正确答案反映真实泛化还是死记硬背"等根本问题停留在推测层面，缺乏证据支撑。

Method: 提出LUMOS诊断框架，利用训练语料完全透明的OLMo 2，将分析锚定在可验证的训练数据暴露上，沿从训练数据暴露到行为输出的因果链追踪知识。包括：测量模型内部对事实的编码可分性、行为表达能力、检索差距随规模的变化，以及在自我反思（含思维链提示）下的表现。

Result: 模型内部以高可分性（84%）编码罕见事实，但行为表达失败（54%），该检索差距随规模扩大而收窄；自我反思时，模型在已训练内容上表现可靠（83%），但在未见内容上骤降至随机基线水平（49%）；该崩塌在思维链提示下依然存在，且思维链反而抬高置信度信号而非改善校准。

Conclusion: 将训练数据轴纳入LLM评估，可将原本推测性的诊断转化为可验证的结论；主张训练数据轴应成为LLM知识评估的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Current analyses of LLMs' parametric knowledge are largely output-centric, drawing conclusions about what a model knows without verifying what it was actually trained on. This leaves fundamental questions, such as whether a correct response reflects genuine generalization or rote memorization, grounded in speculation rather than evidence. To resolve these ambiguities, we introduce LUMOS, a diagnostic framework that traces knowledge along the causal chain from training-data exposure to behavioral output, leveraging OLMo 2 with its fully transparent training corpus. By grounding analysis in verified exposure, we reveal that models internally encode rare facts with high separability (84%) yet fail to express them behaviorally (54%), though this retrieval gap narrows with scale. Furthermore, when models are asked to self-reflect on their own answers, they perform reliably on trained content (83%) but drop to random-baseline levels (49%) on unseen content. This collapse persists even under chain-of-thought prompting, which inflates confidence signals rather than improving calibration. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that incorporating the training-data axis into LLM evaluation transforms speculative diagnoses into verifiable claims, and we advocate that this axis should be a standard component of knowledge assessment in LLMs.

</details>


### [230] [Frequency Is Not Sensitivity Identifying Safety-Sensitive Experts in Sparse MoE LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02910)
*Md Nurul Absar Siddiky,Liuwan Zhu,Yingfei Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验了"路由器梯度敏感性"这一信号能否优于常用的激活频率指标，用于定位稀疏MoE模型中可被抑制以削弱安全拒答行为的关键专家。在五种MoE架构、两种预算设置下，梯度选择在25个条件中的24个里比激活频率带来更多拒答下降，并在全部25个条件中优于随机基线，最强效果出现在OLMoE上（拒答率相对下降73.53%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑制少量被路由的专家即可在不重训的情况下削弱稀疏MoE语言模型的安全行为，因此"抑制哪些专家"本身是一个安全问题。常规答案依据激活频率，但频率衡量的是"使用程度"而非"影响力"，因此作者希望检验一种替代信号——路由器梯度敏感性是否更有效。

Method: 定义路由器梯度敏感性为序列损失对选择专家的门控权重的敏感度。在五种MoE架构上，分别用激活频率和梯度敏感性对专家排序，样本为500条良性提示与500条恶意提示，并在100条留出恶意提示上测量拒答率。设置两种预算：专家数量相等、以及名义恶意路由流量相等（1%–5%）。此外进行同层专家数量匹配的对照实验，并做跨模型探索性分析。

Result: 在两种预算的每一组中，梯度选择的拒答下降幅度在25个条件里有24个超过激活频率，在全部25个条件里超过十次试验的随机均值。最大效应出现在OLMoE：100条提示中的拒答数由34降至9（相对下降73.53%），且输出质量未退化，说明是实质性合规而非生成崩坏。逐层匹配专家数量后，梯度选择仍在25个条件中的23个里优于激活频率，另有两个平局。探索性跨模型分析显示，恶意与良性之间更集中的差距与更强的峰值梯度效应相关（rho=0.90；双侧精确p=0.083，n=5）。

Conclusion: 在本文测试的两种预算下，路由器梯度敏感性作为专家选择信号，相较激活频率能带来更强的拒答削弱效果，且优势在各种架构与预算设定下保持一致，因此结果支持在给定预算下采用梯度选择。作者同时指出跨模型相关性分析仅属探索性质，样本量较小（n=5）。

Abstract: Suppressing a small set of routed experts can weaken the safety behavior of a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model without retraining. Which experts to suppress is therefore a security question, and the usual answer is activation frequency, but frequency measures use, not influence. We test an alternative: router-gradient sensitivity, the sensitivity of the sequence loss to the gate weights that select an expert. Across five MoE architectures, we rank experts by each signal on 500 benign and 500 malicious prompts and measure refusal on 100 held-out malicious prompts under two budgets: equal expert counts and equal nominal malicious routing traffic (1%-5%). Under each of the two budgets, router-gradient selection reduces refusals more than activation in 24 of 25 conditions, and more than a ten-trial random mean in all 25. The largest effect is in OLMoE, where refusals fall from 34 to 9 of 100 prompts (73.53% relative) with no degraded outputs, indicating substantive compliance rather than broken generation. After matching expert counts in every layer, gradient selection still produces greater refusal reduction than activation in 23 of 25 conditions, with two ties. An exploratory cross-model analysis links larger malicious-versus-benign concentration gaps to greater peak gradient effects (rho = 0.90; exact two-sided p = 0.083, n = 5). Together, the results support gradient selection under the tested budgets.

</details>


### [231] [HASTE: Evolving Agent Harnesses Against Emerging Attacks Using Sparse Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02920)
*Xiqiao Xiong,Moxin Li,Zhixin Ma,Ouxiang Li,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE：一种多智能体框架，通过安全规范生成与攻击用例生成的对抗互动，从稀疏威胁证据中演化智能体防护装置（agent harness），降低攻击成功率并保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理防护装置（agent harnesses）通过施加安全约束来阻止不安全行为，是防御的关键环节。然而，新出现的攻击速度远超人工防护装置的适配速度，因此需要自动化演化。但可用于演化防护装置的信号往往十分稀疏，例如威胁报告和预印本中仅有简短描述或少量攻击示例。

Method: 提出 HASTE 多智能体框架，在安全规范生成与攻击用例生成之间构建对抗性互动：安全规范引导防护装置更新以应对已识别的安全漏洞，攻击用例则在每次更新后探测仍存在的漏洞，并将评估结果反馈回上述两个过程，从而实现基于稀疏证据的防护装置演化，并泛化至初始证据之外的攻击。

Result: 在多种骨干模型、攻击类型和证据形式上的实验表明，HASTE 能持续降低攻击成功率，同时保持良性任务效用。

Conclusion: HASTE 通过对抗式安全规范与攻击用例协同生成，有效利用稀疏威胁证据驱动防护装置自动演化，在提升安全性的同时维持实用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agent harnesses play a critical role in defenses by enforcing safety constraints to prevent unsafe actions. However, rapidly emerging attacks outpace manual harness adaptation, motivating automated harness evolution. Yet the signals available for harness evolution are often sparse, such as brief descriptions or a few attack examples in threat reports and preprints. To address this limitation, we introduce HASTE, a multi-agent framework that evolves agent harnesses from sparse threat evidence through an adversarial interplay between safety-specification generation and attack-case generation. Safety specifications guide harness updates toward addressing identified safety vulnerabilities, while attack cases probe for remaining safety vulnerabilities after each update. By feeding evaluation outcomes back into both processes, HASTE enables harness evolution against emerging attacks beyond the initially observed evidence. Experimental results across multiple backbone models, attack types, and evidence forms show that HASTE consistently reduces attack success rates while preserving benign-task utility. The code is available at https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE.

</details>


### [232] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段PU排序框架，用于语言模型安全监控中的误报审计，在无需误报安全标签的前提下显著提升误报识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全监控器会产生大量误报，消耗审查资源并削弱信任；由于真误报与真实警报在原始监控分数中混杂，确定可靠阈值仍需大量人工核验。实践中仅有少量经核验安全的未报警轨迹，而警报样本无标签，因此误报审计天然可建模为正样本-未标注（PU）排序问题。核心挑战是监控器引导的选择偏差：观测到的安全参考是被监控器接受的，而真正关注的隐藏安全警报恰是被其错误标记的样本，导致观测正样本对目标正样本代表性差。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）Trust-aware PU Supervision，将安全参考自适应迁移至警报域，并保护可能的误报免受过度负压；2）Reliability-gated Rank Distillation，将多个PU参考模型的一致排序偏好整合到单一学生模型中。随后通过Consensus-guided Structural Refinement，利用分层安全参考支持、警报关系与预测参考共识来优化学生排序。该框架拟合时不需要警报安全标签，且不修改底层监控器。

Result: 在主流安全监控器上，方法取得0.6444的macro AUPRC，较八个PU基线高出5.27–16.98个绝对百分点；与最强PU基线PULDA相比，在5%审查预算下多恢复33.3%的误报。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解监控器引导的选择偏差，在无警报安全标签条件下实现了更优的误报排序，为安全监控的误报审计提供了实用且不侵入的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [233] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE系统，通过度量协议与在线编译算法解决GUI程序编译中成本不确定与复用未知两大难题，在预算约束下使总成本不超过纯智能体执行的1+ε倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将智能体重复执行的GUI流程编译为程序可降低token成本，但决定何时编译存在两个挑战：编译成本不确定（尝试可能需修复甚至失败），未来复用情况未知（任务可能停止到达或GUI漂移导致程序失效）。

Method: PACE包含度量协议与在线编译算法。度量协议记录成功与失败的编译成本，并在匹配的任务输入上比较智能体与程序的执行成本，以估计单次节省与回收次数；在线算法结合历史任务到达与编译结果，将估计的未来节省与含失败尝试的编译成本比较，并在允许执行或编译前检查其开销是否超出由观测到的任务到达所确定的累计预算。

Result: 在所述动作成本假设下，每次任务到达后的总成本至多为全部任务均用智能体执行成本的1+ε倍。成功编译的估计回收次数（不含源智能体运行）为2–16次。使用记录的任务到达进行模拟，PACE平均降低token成本：相比ReAct为17.3%，相比AutoRPA改编为24.9%，相比ToolPro改编为17.3%（ε=0.25）。

Conclusion: PACE通过度量编译成本与复用收益并施加累计预算约束，在理论上保证接近纯智能体执行的竞争比，在模拟中显著降低token开销，验证了按回收期感知进行在线编译决策的有效性。

Abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+ε$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($ε=0.25$).

</details>


### [234] [Continual Graph Memory for Mathematical Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02945)
*Junyi Zhang,Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiachen Lu,Zhi Zhang,Xinjie He,Hyunsik Chae,Ethan Ji,Alexander K Taylor,Vigyan Sahai,Yiwen Kou,Kai-Wei Chang,Raghu Meka,Nanyun Peng,Amit Sahai,Terence Tao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ansatz——一个基于"持续图记忆"（Continual Graph Memory）的数学研究智能体，通过图结构组织长时程证明搜索过程并复用跨问题探索知识，在十项研究任务上全部完成证明，并独立解决了多个开放数学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用前沿智能体框架攻克数学研究问题已成为推进数学发展的有效手段，但前沿数学问题往往需要大量智能体长期并行工作来构造证明，产生海量中间证明结果。如何在长时程证明搜索过程中组织这些中间结果，并复用此前探索所获得的知识，仍是主要挑战。

Method: Ansatz 围绕"持续图记忆"构建，这是一个基于图、可演化、跨问题的数学研究记忆系统。具体包括：统一的图记忆，表示所有中间探索结果（事实、计划、反例）及其相互关系的显式边；依赖感知检索，提供精确的局部上下文；证据敏感策展器，更新研究前沿并从先前尝试中提炼经验；范围化召回，将早期陈述与负面发现用于局部重证，而非不加批判地复用。

Result: 实验覆盖全部十个"First Proof Second Batch"问题，并包含四项组件研究。Ansatz 在全部十项研究任务上实现闭合（closure），展现了维持和恢复长时程数学搜索的能力。此外，Ansatz 在无人工干预下给出了 Jamison caterpillar 猜想以及 Erdős 问题 289、348、488 的解答，并在若干开放问题上取得部分进展。

Conclusion: Ansatz 通过持续图记忆有效组织长时程证明搜索并复用跨问题知识，能够持续开展数学搜索，并在解决开放数学研究问题方面展现出较强能力。

Abstract: Using frontier agent harnesses to tackle mathematical research problems has emerged as an effective means of advancing mathematics. However, solving frontier problems in mathematics may require a massive number of agents working in parallel for extended periods to construct proofs, thereby generating an enormous volume of intermediate proof results. Organizing these intermediate results throughout a long-horizon proof-search process and reusing knowledge gained from prior explorations remain major challenges. We present Ansatz, a mathematical research agent built around Continual Graph Memory, a graph-based, evolvable, cross-problem mathematical research memory system that explicitly organizes the entire proof search process and reuses information from exploration trajectories of previous problems. Specifically, we develop a unified graph memory that represents all intermediate exploration results, including facts, plans, and counterexamples, together with edges that explicitly represent the relationships among them; dependency-aware retrieval supplies precisely targeted local context; an evidence-sensitive curator updates the research frontier and distills lessons from prior attempts; and scoped recall surfaces earlier statements and negative findings for local re-proving rather than uncritical reuse. Experiments cover runs across all ten First Proof Second Batch problems, together with four component studies. Ansatz reports closure on all ten research tasks, demonstrating its ability to sustain and resume long-horizon mathematical search. Beyond these problems, Ansatz also produces solutions to the Jamison caterpillar conjecture and Erdős Problems 289, 348, and 488 without human intervention, and makes partial progress on several open problems, illustrating its strong ability to solve open mathematical research problems.

</details>


### [235] [Personalized Automatic Speech Recognition for a Dysarthric and Tracheostomic Speaker using Artificial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03017)
*David Nadrchal,Monorama Swain,Florian Schmid,Gerhard Widmer,Paul Primus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对一名患有永久性气管造口及重度构音障碍的捷克语使用者，构建个性化ASR系统，并公开33小时数据集，提出基于Whisper Base的多阶段训练流程，CER相对降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该说话者的语音对未经训练的听者完全不可懂，属于严重障碍语音，现有通用ASR难以适用；同时亟需验证在重度受损语音条件下是否仍可构建有实用价值的识别系统。

Method: 发布33小时标注数据集，采用面向高参与度与对话真实性的“人工对话”采集协议；以Whisper Base为基座，构建多阶段微调流程：标准捷克语语音→声学模拟的气管造口语音→该说话者本人数据。

Result: 在脚本对话、问答和自发对话三种近实时场景下评估，字符错误率（CER）相较Whisper Base基线实现50%的相对下降，并在孤立话语的声学识别上超过其助手们的平均识别准确率。

Conclusion: 即便面对严重受损语音，仍可获得具有实用帮助价值的ASR系统，该结论由定量结果及说话者本人的反馈共同支持。

Abstract: This work presents an automatic speech recognition (ASR) system personalized for a Czech speaker with a permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria rendering their speech unintelligible to untrained listeners. We release a public dataset containing 33 annotated hours of the speaker's speech, collected using a novel "artificial conversation" protocol designed for high engagement and dialogue realism. We propose a multi-stage training pipeline based on Whisper Base: fine-tuning on standard Czech speech, acoustically simulated tracheostomic speech, and the speaker's data. We evaluate the system across three near real-time scenarios: scripted conversations, question answering, and spontaneous dialogue, achieving a 50\% relative reduction in Character Error Rate compared to Whisper Base baseline and surpassing the average recognition accuracy of their assistants in acoustic recognition of isolated utterances. We demonstrate that even for severely impeded speech, a helpful ASR is achievable, as evidenced by the quantitative results and the feedback from the speaker.

</details>


### [236] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PROVE-REC 框架，通过两阶段（Pass A 生成偏好证明、Pass B 仅凭证明预测）与两类验证目标，解决 LLM 推荐中"理由与实际所用信息脱节"的 grounding-influence gap 问题，实验性能提升最高 7.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能从交互历史与评论中推断用户偏好，但其生成的推荐理由未必反映真正用于推荐决策的信息：偏好声明可能缺乏所选证据的有力支撑，也可能对最终排序影响甚微。作者将这两类失效统称为 grounding-influence gap，即理由与证据之间的可溯源性不足、理由与推荐结果之间的因果影响力不足。

Method: 提出 PROVE-REC 通用框架。Pass A 将完整的 pre-target 历史压缩为紧凑的偏好证明，包含正向偏好声明与规避性声明，并各自关联所选证据条目。Pass B 仅使用该证明及其证据预测下一物品，强制推荐器不得绕过推理路径。验证机制包括两项：以"遮蔽所选证据"与"遮蔽可比对照条目"的效果差异检验 evidence-to-proof grounding；以移除某条偏好声明后目标物品排序间隔的下降幅度检验 proof-to-recommendation influence。并引入排序保持目标，以保留完整历史中的有效信息。

Result: 在广泛的真实数据集上，PROVE-REC 持续优于强序列模型、生成式模型及 LLM 增强基线，提升幅度最高达 7.45%。受控消融实验验证了两阶段架构与两项验证目标的有效性。此外，PROVE-REC 生成的声明在历史证据中具有更强的可溯源性，对推荐结果影响更大，同时保持排序质量。

Conclusion: PROVE-REC 通过可验证的偏好推理，缓解了 LLM 推荐中理由与依据脱节的问题，在提升推荐性能的同时改善了理由的可解释性与因果影响力，为可验证的 LLM 推荐提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [237] [CreateScore: Domain-Theory-Informed Bayesian Routing for LLM-Based CV Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02972)
*Rupsa Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreateScore是一种领域理论启发的贝叶斯网络，用于简历筛选中的准则级LLM路由：以不确定性决定本地8B模型处理或升级至120B模型。在200份合成简历上，77.7%决策本地解决，token使用降低65.2%，但与参考模型的一致率仅从32.8%提升至42.6%且不显著；不确定性信号无法识别8B出错处（AUROC 0.47）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用高能力模型对每位候选人、每条准则逐一评分成本高昂，因此需要一种仅在必要时升级的级联（cascade）路由机制，以在保持评分质量的同时降低推理成本。

Method: 构建手工指定DAG与Dirichlet-multinomial条件概率表的贝叶斯网络，将简历证据转为后验不确定性（仅做标准贝叶斯条件化，非因果推断）。低不确定性由本地8B模型判定，高不确定性升级至120B参考模型；升级阈值在训练折上校准（目标70%本地解决）后冻结。实验用200份合成Data Science简历（139训练、61测试，5条准则）。

Result: 305条准则决策中77.7%（237条）本地解决；相比全部由120B裁决的参照条件，token使用减少65.2%，精确一致率由8B单用的32.8%升至42.6%（95% CI 31.0-55.1%），但n=61时提升不显著。不确定性信号未能定位8B错误：升级项与参考不一致率16.2%，本地解决项19.4%（AUROC 0.47，95% CI 0.39-0.56），与随机无异。另记录了因截断的推理模型输出被静默替换为本地标签而导致早前评估失效的问题。

Conclusion: CreateScore可作为一种可审计的成本削减机制，但尚不能作为针对性的错误检测器，也不是自主招聘系统。建议为级联式评估建立防护措施（如禁止静默替换截断输出）。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support rubric-based screening of CVs, but applying a high-capability model to every candidate and criterion is costly. We present CreateScore, a domain-theory-informed Bayesian network for criterion-level LLM routing.
